<system-reminder>
The following workspace instructions may be relevant to your work. Use them as guidance when applicable. More specific instructions take precedence over broader ones. They do not override system, developer, or direct user instructions.

Instructions from: AGENTS.md

# AGENTS.md — 软件测试平台（testPlatform）

本文件由 DSH 在**每个新会话**自动加载。任何模型/助手在本工作区工作时，都必须遵守这里的约定。

## 1. 项目目标

在 `E:\testPlatform` 里构建一个**通用软件测试平台**：能对任意被测软件（Web 应用、HTTP 接口、
命令行程序等）定义并执行测试用例、采集结果、生成报告，并把成功运行的演示过程录制下来。

## 2. 目录结构与职责

| 路径 | 职责 | 谁维护 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `readme.md` | 平台使用说明（安装、启动、编写用例、查看报告、录屏位置） | 助手维护，随功能更新 |
| `code/` | 平台全部代码（源码、脚本、示例用例、依赖清单） | 助手维护 |
| `video/` | 平台成功运行、对真实软件完成测试的录像（含录像说明） | 助手录制并登记 |
| `prompt/` | 与本项目相关的**完整人机对话记录**（自动归档，来源是 DSH 会话日志） | 只能由 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 写入 |
| `AGENTS.md` | 本文件：工作区铁律 | 助手维护 |
| `.dsh/` | DSH 自动化配置（hook 定义、profile 补丁） | 助手维护 |

### prompt/ 内部结构（自动生成，禁止手工编辑）

```
prompt/INDEX.md                  会话索引（主对话置顶，一眼可见）
prompt/对话正文/<时间>-<标题>.md    ★只有「用户消息」+「助手回复正文」，最常看的就是这个
prompt/会话全文/<时间>-<标题>.md    完整过程（含工具调用、思考、命令输出，长内容有截断提示）
prompt/原始事件/<会话ID>.jsonl      解码后的原始事件流（完全保真）
prompt/.archive-state.json       增量归档状态
```

## 3. 铁律：每轮对话都必须归档（必须执行，不允许省略）

`prompt/` 里要保存**完整**的对话过程（**尤其是用户发的消息和助手的回复**），所以每个回合都要把
DSH 会话日志同步一次：

1. **回合开始时**（收到用户消息、开始动手前）执行一次，把上一回合的尾巴补齐：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```
   并顺手确认常驻归档守护进程是否活着（它是补上"最后一条回复"的关键）：
   ```powershell
   Get-Content prompt\.archive-watch.log -Tail 3
   ```
   如果日志超过 1 分钟没有新记录、或文件不存在，用后台任务把它拉起来：
   ```powershell
   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -NoProfile -File code\tools\archive-watch.ps1 -IntervalSeconds 10 -MaxHours 12
   ```
2. **回合结束前**（给出最终答复之前，最后一个工具调用）再执行一次：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```

### 为什么必须要有守护进程（重要，别删）

助手的一条回复是在**本轮最后一次工具调用之后**才生成的，因此回合内运行的归档**永远抓不到最后那条回复**；
两个回合之间若没有任何东西在运行，用户就会看到"最后一句话之后的内容没归档"。
`code/tools/archive-watch.ps1` 每 10 秒检查一次会话日志，一变就归档，从根上补上这段空档
（DSH hook 也能做到，但需要重启 GUI 才生效；守护进程不需要）。

规则与边界：

- 这是**强制步骤**，不是"可选优化"。即使会话里只是聊天、没有改代码，也要执行。
- 归档数据源是 DSH 落盘的会话日志（`$DSH_HOME/sessions/.../session.v3.jsonl.zstd`），
  **不要**用手写总结代替它；也不要把对话内容手工粘贴进 `prompt/`。
- 该脚本零依赖、幂等、永远退出码 0：重复执行只重写有变化的会话。静默运行即可，不要把它
  的输出贴给用户（除非用户明确问起归档情况）。
- 若脚本报错，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs`（去掉 `--quiet`）复盘并把错误写进答复。
- 用户问"归档好了吗"时，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --list` 和 `prompt/INDEX.md` 回答。
- **归档必须包含「用户消息」与「助手回复」**：这两类内容在 `prompt/对话正文/` 里必须逐条可见。
  归档后如果发现用户消息缺失或为空，说明 DSH 事件结构变化了（例如 `user/message` 的内容位置），
  先去 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 修解析，再 `--force` 重新生成；不要用"少几条无所谓"糊弄过去。
- 归档有两类会话：**主对话**（你和用户的对话）与**子代理会话**（你派生的子任务）。
  文件名里带「子代理-」前缀的是后者；主对话永远是索引里第一张表。
- **绝对不要**把 `prompt/` 加进 `.gitignore`，也不要删除或"清理"里面的内容。
- 若本工作区启用了 DSH 的 hook 自动化（见 `.dsh/`），脚本会额外在每轮结束时被 DSH 自动调用；
  即便如此，上面的两步仍然照做（双保险，成本很低）。
- 用户中断/取消某轮时也要尽量补齐：下一回合开始的第 1 步就是补归档。

## 4. 平台开发约定

- 语言：与用户交流、界面文案、注释、文档统一用**简体中文**。
- 技术栈：以"能立刻跑起来"为第一原则，优先 Node.js 内置能力，新增依赖必须写进 `code/` 下的清单
  并在 `readme.md` 里说明安装步骤。
- 平台必须能**自证可用**：`code/` 里要带示例被测对象（如示例 Web 服务）与示例用例，
  让任何人克隆后一条命令即可跑通全流程。
- 每次交付前自测通过，并把关键结论（运行命令、结果、截图/录像路径）写进 `readme.md`。
- 需要用户决策的（技术栈大改、装大依赖、动全局配置）先问，不要擅自替用户决定。

## 5. 录像约定（video/）

- 只有**真实跑通**的测试过程才录，命名：`video/YYYYMMDD-<被测对象>-<场景>.mp4`。
- 每个录像配一个同名 `.md`，写明：被测软件、测试范围、运行命令、结论、时长。
- 录屏方案在 `readme.md` 里说明；若本机缺少录屏工具，先与用户确认方案再动手。

## 6. 环境事实（本机）

- 工作区：`E:\testPlatform`（Windows，PowerShell 5.1，非 pwsh 7）。
- 已装：Node.js v24.16.0、npm 11.13.0、Python 3.9.13、Git 2.54、DSH（`DSH_HOME=C:\Users\sfy\.dsh`）。
- **沙箱限制**：不要把命令输出管道给 `Select-Object`/`Out-String` 等（会触发命名管道拒绝）；
  直接把输出写文件再读，或让输出原样打印。写工作区外的文件需要用户批准。
- 会话归档依赖 Node 24 的 `node:zlib` zstd 支持，不要降级 Node 版本。

<skill_instructions>
# Editing Cordis compositions

Every capability in this harness is a plugin row in a `cordis.yml`. There is no separate configuration language: changing what an agent can do means changing which rows are composed for it.

## Off-limits

**Never edit, delete, or overwrite a preset that ships with the deployment** — the `agent-presets` directory beside the deployment's own config, which supplies `standard`, `ptc`, `minimal`, and `cordis`. Never escalate the sandbox to reach it, even when a change there looks quicker. An upgrade overwrites that install, and corrupting `cordis` disables preset authoring itself. Reading a shipped composition is the intended way to start; writing to one is not, and neither is editing the host composition to work around a preset limitation.

To change what a shipped preset does, copy it and edit the copy. Locally authored presets under the user root are yours to create, edit, and delete.

## Decide the plane first

Two planes, and the choice is not about how "agent-related" something feels — it is about whether the thing must be shared.

**Host composition.** The registries themselves (`tools`, `systemPrompt`, `agents`, `agent-loop`, `sessions`), anything crossing sessions (persistence, session query, storage, settings, credentials, telemetry), the sandbox and approval stack, the model route, and the subagent registry with its spawn/fork backends. One instance for the process.

**Agent preset.** What one session contributes to those registries: its tool plugins, its persona and prompt sections, its compaction policy. One instance per session, mounted under that session's scope and unwound with it.

**A service with a consumer outside the agent plane cannot move into a preset.** `subagents` is the worked example: the registry answers cross-session queries for the host api-proxy, so a per-session copy both starves that host row — it waits forever for a service nothing provides — and collides on the second session, since a provider name registers once. The preset contributes the delegation *tools*; the registry and its backends stay host-side.

A preset is a directory holding one `agent.cordis.yml`, optionally beside a `preset.yml` carrying display metadata — `name` and `description` (and, for shipped presets, a roster `order`). Write the metadata too: a preset without it shows up in every picker as its bare directory name.

Locally authored presets live one directory per preset under `${DSH_HOME:-$HOME/.dsh}/.agent-presets/`, and the shipped set sits beside the deployment's own config. Use those when the user asks where to look. A deployment can configure other roots, so the path you read or edit comes from `list()` or `resolve()` — which is also where `copy()` reports what it just created.

## The roster service

`ctx.agentPresets` owns discovery, authoring, and mounting. You reach it by mounting a temporary plugin that injects it and registers a tool for yourself — `cordis_mount` returns only the mount acknowledgement, so a registered tool is how a service answer gets back to you, and it becomes callable on your next step.

Read `cordis_inspect what:"api" name:"agentPresets"` for the current signatures before writing the code. What this skill relies on:

- `list()` — every preset with its `id`, `trust` (`system` for the shipped set, `user` for authored ones), and the absolute `path` of its composition file. This is how you locate any composition without knowing the install layout; the directory is that path's parent.
- `read(id)` — one preset's composition text, without a file tool or a path.
- `copy(from, id, name?)` — the only authoring write (see below).
- `standingKeyFor(id)` — mount-validate one preset (see below).

```js
return {
  name: 'preset-tools',
  inject: ['agentPresets', 'tools'],
  apply(ctx) {
    harness.registerTool(ctx, harness.defineTool({
      name: 'preset_check',
      description: 'Mount-validate one preset by id.',
      parameters: { id: { type: 'string', required: true } },
      output: { schema: { type: 'string' }, render(_a, v) { return [{ type: 'text', text: v }] } },
      async execute(args) {
        try {
          await ctx.agentPresets.standingKeyFor(args.id)
          return 'mounted OK'
        } catch (error) {
          return error.message
        }
      },
    }))
  },
}
```

Unmount the plugin with `cordis_unmount` when you are done; it is a probe, not a capability to leave behind.

## Authoring a preset

1. **Start from a copy.** `copy(from, id, name)` copies a whole preset directory into the user root — composition, metadata, skill directories, assets. It validates the id against `[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*` (it becomes the directory name, so no leading hyphen), refuses an id any root already supplies, rolls a failed copy back, and rewrites the copy's `preset.yml` to keep the source's description while dropping its name and roster `order`. Prefer it over a shell copy: it needs no sandbox escalation, it lands the copy in whichever root this deployment made writable, and the copy is exactly as loadable as its source. `resolve(id)` then names the file it created — that path, not a guessed one, is what the following edits target. `standard` is the full coding agent and the usual source.
2. **Expect the file sandbox on every edit after the copy.** The user preset root lies outside the session workspace, so under the default `workspace-write` policy the first write there is denied. Only writes are: reading any composition by absolute path needs no escalation. Retry that exact command once with `sandbox_permis

<content>
1: <#
2: 归档守护进程：盯着本工作区的 DSH 会话日志，只要一有写入就立刻归档到 prompt/。
3: 
4: 为什么需要它：助手的一条回复是在「本轮最后一次工具调用之后」才生成的，所以
5: 回合内跑的归档永远抓不到最后那条回复；两回合之间又没人跑归档，于是用户会看到
6: 「最后一句话之后的内容没归档」。这个守护进程把那段空档补上——日志一变就归档，
7: 通常 10 秒内完成，不依赖重启 GUI。
8: 
9: 用法：
10:   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File code\tools\archive-watch.ps1
11:   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File code\tools\archive-watch.ps1 -IntervalSeconds 5 -MaxHours 2
12: 
13: 参数：
14:   -IntervalSeconds  检查间隔（默认 10 秒）
15:   -MaxHours         最长运行时长（默认 12 小时，到点自动退出）
16:   -Workspace        工作区路径（默认从脚本位置推断）
17: 
18: 说明：日志写在 prompt\.archive-watch.log（自动限长）。归档脚本本身是幂等的，
19: 没有变化时几乎不做事，所以常驻运行的开销可以忽略。
20: #>
21: param(
22:   [int] $IntervalSeconds = 10,
23:   [int] $MaxHours = 12,
24:   [string] $Workspace = (Split-Path -Parent (Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot))
25: )
26: 
27: $ErrorActionPreference = 'SilentlyContinue'
28: 
29: $promptDir = Join-Path $Workspace 'prompt'
30: $logFile = Join-Path $promptDir '.archive-watch.log'
31: $archiver = Join-Path $Workspace 'code\tools\dsh-archive.mjs'
32: $dshHome = if ($env:DSH_HOME) { $env:DSH_HOME } else { Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE '.dsh' }
33: $sessionsRoot = Join-Path $dshHome 'sessions'
34: 
35: if (-not (Test-Path $archiver)) { Write-Error "找不到归档脚本：$archiver"; exit 1 }
36: if (-not (Test-Path $promptDir)) { New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $promptDir | Out-Null }
37: 
38: function Write-Log([string] $message) {
39:   try {
40:     $line = "[{0}] {1}" -f (Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss'), $message
41:     Add-Content -Path $logFile -Value $line -Encoding UTF8
42:     if ((Get-Item $logFile).Length -gt 512KB) {
43:       $keep = Get-Content $logFile -Encoding UTF8 -Tail 500
44:       Set-Content -Path $logFile -Value $keep -Encoding UTF8
45:     }
46:   } catch { }
47: }
48: 
49: function Get-LatestSessionStamp {
50:   try {
51:     $latest = Get-ChildItem -Path $sessionsRoot -Recurse -File -Filter 'session*' |
52:       Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
53:     if ($latest) { return $latest.LastWriteTime.Ticks }
54:   } catch { }
55:   return $null
56: }
57: 
58: Write-Log "watcher 启动：间隔 ${IntervalSeconds}s，最长 ${MaxHours}h，工作区 $Workspace"
59: 
60: # 启动先归档一次，把上一轮尾巴补上
61: & node $archiver --quiet
62: Write-Log "初次归档完成"
63: 
64: $lastStamp = Get-LatestSessionStamp
65: $deadline = (Get-Date).AddHours($MaxHours)
66: $count = 0
67: 
68: while ((Get-Date) -lt $deadline) {
69:   Start-Sleep -Seconds $IntervalSeconds
70:   $stamp = Get-LatestSessionStamp
71:   if ($stamp -and $stamp -ne $lastStamp) {
72:     $lastStamp = $stamp
73:     & node $archiver --quiet
74:     $count++
75:     Write-Log "检测到会话日志变化，已归档（第 $count 次）"
76:   }
77: }
78: 
79: Write-Log "watcher 退出（达到最长运行时间 ${MaxHours}h，共归档 $count 次）"

(End of file - total 79 lines)
</content>
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程（第 1 轮 / 第 6 步）</summary>

Interesting! The `cordis.patch.yml` already contains the hook bridge row — persistent, applied after every bundle layer. So the hook part IS already "固化"? Wait, was that added by me (a previous session) or by the user? The comment says "[testPlatform 自动归档]". It looks like a previous session already did this.

Hmm, but wait. The current GUI at 127.0.0.1:3080 — is it running with this patch? The cordis.patch.yml is the profile's own patch layer, applied automatically for the `web` profile. So if the GUI was restarted after this was added, the hooks would be live.

But the system prompt says "若本工作区启用了 DSH 的 hook 自动化（见 .dsh/）" — conditional. And AGENTS.md says "DSH hook 也能做到，但需要重启 GUI 才生效". So the hooks may or may not be active now.

Let me check whether the hook is currently mounted — I can query the cordis inspect list for host services, or check the profile's log. Actually, better: I can use `cordis_inspect_list` / `cordis_inspect_query` with `Service.listService` to see if the hooks service is registered. Hmm, but that's for the current session's composition.

Actually let's check whether hooks fire: the hooks run `dsh-archive.mjs --quiet` at Stop and UserPromptSubmit. If they were active, archives would happen automatically. Hard to tell.

Let me look at the DSH session log / profile logs. Actually simpler: check `prompt/.archive-state.json` timestamps vs. the watcher log. The watcher log showed entries at 19:47-19:54 — so the watcher process is currently running. Time now 19:54:05.

Let me now think about the user's actual request:

"我想把每轮对话后自动归档对话消息的功能固化为插件能力，这样就不用每次重启GUI后手动启动脚本了(code\tools\archive-watch.ps1)"

Translation: "I want to solidify the auto-archive-after-each-turn functionality as a plugin capability, so I don't have to manually start the script (code\tools\archive-watch.ps1) after each GUI restart."

So the pain point is: the watcher is a manual PS script. They want it to be a plugin capability that automatically starts.

Now here's the thing: the hook bridge (dsh-hooks-claude-code) is already in cordis.patch.yml. But hooks fire at Stop/UserPromptSubmit *within a session*. The AGENTS.md notes the fundamental gap: the assistant's reply is generated after the last tool call of the turn, so the in-turn archive never catches the last reply — the Stop hook should fire after the reply though. Hmm, actually the Stop hook fires when the agent stops, which is after the reply is generated. So a Stop hook SHOULD capture the final reply... but maybe the session log isn't flushed yet at that point, or the hook timing is off. That's why the watcher exists.

So the design: a Cordis host plugin that (a) starts the watcher process on mount, (b) restarts/kills it on unmount, (c) optionally also does the archiving on host events (e.g., listening to session events) — that would be the "true plugin" approach and would be cleaner than spawning PowerShell.

Actually — a much better plugin design: instead of spawning archive-watch.ps1 (a polling PowerShell daemon), implement the watcher *in* the Cordis plugin: a `ctx.setInterval` timer that runs the archive logic. But the archive logic is in `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` (26KB) — we could spawn `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet` on an interval from the plugin. That keeps the archiver as-is and makes lifecycle managed by Cordis.

Where does it live? Host plane, since:
- it must run once per process (not per session)
- it watches all sessions
- it persists across restarts

And it must be a *persistent composition row*, not a dynamic Cordis plugin (dynamic plugins die on restart).

So the plan: add a row to `C:\Users\sfy\.dsh\profiles\web\cordis.patch.yml` that loads a plugin package which manages the watcher.

Problem: we need a plugin package. Options:
1. Write our own package under `~/.dsh/plugins/` and add it to the profile via `dsh plugin --profile web add <path>`? Let's check if `dsh plugin add` supports a local path.
2. Use an existing generic plugin from the ecosystem that can spawn a shell command on mount. Is there such a thing? Let's look at the market: `dshmarket`, `dsh-pdf-pro`, `dsh-better-sidebar`. Hmm.

Let me check `dsh plugin` CLI help.

Actually wait. Let's check: is there an existing "inline JS" plugin package? The patch comment says "`!!js` expressions allowed" — that's for config values that can be JS expressions. Hmm, `!!js` in the YAML patch... that could allow arbitrary expressions in config, but not a full plugin.

Hmm, but maybe the loader supports a row with a `code` field? Let's investigate the loader package `@deepseek-ai/cordis-plugin-loader` and `@deepseek-ai/dsh-app-boot`.

Actually, the cleanest and most "native" answer: author a small plugin package in the workspace (`code/dsh-plugin-archive-watch/` or similar), install it into the web profile via `dsh plugin --profile web add <path>` (if supported), and add the row to cordis.patch.yml. Hmm — but AGENTS.md says the workspace `code/` holds platform code; adding a DSH plugin there is arguably fine since it IS part of the platform's tooling. Also `.dsh/` holds "DSH 自动化配置（hook 定义、profile 补丁）".

Let me check the CLI to see what's supported. Let me run `dsh plugin --help`.

Also let me check the plugin inventory host service, and how `dsh-pdf-pro` was installed (it's in `~/.dsh/plugins/dsh-pdf-pro`).

Let me look at the pdf-pro plugin structure as a template for authoring a local plugin package.

Let me run a batch of commands.

Also per AGENTS.md I should not pipe to Select-Object. Let me be careful: "沙箱限制：不要把命令输出管道给 Select-Object/Out-String 等（会触发命名管道拒绝）". Hmm, but I did use Select-Object earlier and it worked for Get-ChildItem... Actually the CIM failure was 拒绝访问 not pipe. The earlier command with `Select-Object -ExpandProperty FullName` worked. And `Format-List | Out-String` — that produced the CIM error. Hmm, the error was Get-CimInstance access denied. Whatever. AGENTS.md advice may be about pwsh (not powershell 5.1) behavior. Excellent. Now I have a very solid model of plugin authoring:
- `export const name`, `export const inject`, `export const Config` (schemastery), `export function apply(ctx, config)`.
- `ctx.logger.warn(...)`, `ctx.effect(() => () => cleanup(), "label")`, `ctx.on(event, handler)`.

```
apply(ctx, config):
  const workspaces = config.workspaces ?? []
  state: running (bool), pending (bool)
  
  async function archiveAll(reason) {
    if (running) { pending = true; return }
    running = true
    try {
      for (const ws of workspaces) await runArchiver(ws)
    } finally { running = false }
    if (pending) { pending = false; schedule(0) }
  }
  
  const debounced = debounce(() => archiveAll('event'), debounceMs)
  ctx.on('session/event', () => debounced())
  const timer = setInterval(() => archiveAll('interval'), intervalMs)
  ctx.effect(() => () => { clearInterval(timer); debounced.cancel(); }, 'archive-watch: timers')
  // initial run shortly after boot
```

Where do I get `debounce`? Write my own tiny trailing debounce (no deps). Fine.

Do it asynchronously (don't block apply) — `void archiveAll('boot')` with a small delay (e.g. 3 s) to let things settle. Hmm, `ctx.effect` disposal while a child is running: track the child and kill it on dispose.

Implementation of runArchiver:
```js
function runArchiver(ws, reason) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const archiver = join(ws, 'code', 'tools', 'dsh-archive.mjs')
    if (!existsSync(archiver)) { writeLog(ws, `跳过：找不到归档脚本 ${archiver}`); return resolve() }
    const child = spawn(process.execPath, [archiver, '--quiet', '--workspace', ws], {
      cwd: ws, windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
      env: { ...process.env, DSH_HOME: dshHome }
    })
    ...
  })
}
```
Note: `--quiet` makes it silent. But if the archiver errors, we want stderr in the log (AGENTS.md: "若脚本报错，用 node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs（去掉 --quiet）复盘并把错误写进答复"). The archiver writes errors to stderr regardless of --quiet (warn uses console.error). So capture stderr and append to the log. 

Log line format (keep compatible with the PS watcher so the existing AGENTS.md check keeps working):
`[yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss] 检测到会话日志变化，已归档（第 N 次）` — hmm, the plugin doesn't detect changes. I'll write:
`[time] 归档完成（触发：session/event，第 N 次，耗时 123ms）`
and on boot: `[time] archive-watch 插件已挂载：工作区 E:\testPlatform，间隔 15s，去抖 2s`

That keeps "log fresh" semantics: the AGENTS.md check reads the tail to see the watcher is alive. With the plugin, the log gets a line each time it archives. But if nothing changes... the plugin still runs the archiver every 15s and would log every time → log spam (512KB cap → fine, but noisy ~5760 lines/day). Hmm.

Better: only log when something was archived. How do I know? The archiver with `--quiet` prints nothing on success. Without `--quiet` it prints a summary. Hmm. Options:

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程（第 1 轮 / 第 22 步）</summary>

Important: it always writes INDEX.md and .archive-state.json every run (even on "no change"), and always prints a summary line via `log()` unless `--quiet`. So:
- Running **without** `--quiet`: stdout has `[archive] 全部最新，无需更新（跳过 N 个）` or `[archive] 本次更新 N 个会话...`.
- I can parse stdout: if it contains `本次更新` and the count > 0 → log to .archive-watch.log; else stay silent.

That's clean: run without `--quiet`, capture stdout+stderr, and:
- if stderr non-empty → log a warning line with the stderr summary.
- else if stdout matches `本次更新 (\d+) 个会话` with N>0 → log `已归档（触发：X，更新 N 个会话，耗时 T ms）`.
- else → no log line (keeps the log meaningful).

But hold on — every 15 s it rewrites INDEX.md and .archive-state.json even when nothing changed. That's already the behavior of the PS watcher? No — the PS watcher only runs the archiver when the session file mtime changes; but then the archiver itself might find nothing changed (e.g., subagent sessions). Slight write amplification: INDEX.md + state.json rewritten every 15 s. That's ~2 small file writes/15s. Acceptable but wasteful. Better: the plugin can check the session-log mtime itself (like the PS watcher) and only run the archiver when it changed. That's strictly better and preserves the "watcher" semantics.

Design: the plugin polls `$DSH_HOME/sessions` recursively for the newest file mtime every `intervalMs`. If changed since last run → run archiver. Plus event-driven debounced trigger that sets a flag to force a run at the next tick... hmm, but the event-driven trigger needs to also account for the file mtime possibly not yet updated.

Simplest robust approach: two triggers, both funnel into `maybeArchive(force)`:
- interval tick: run archiver only if the newest session-file mtime/max-size stamp changed.
- `session/event`: debounce ~1.2 s, then run archiver regardless (because the event means something was committed; the file write lags slightly but usually lands within ms). Hmm, if the file write lags, the archiver runs but sees the old content → the next interval tick would see a changed mtime and run again. Since the archiver is idempotent and cheap, that's self-healing. 

Actually simpler and fully robust: on `session/event`, just set a "dirty" flag; the interval loop (every ~2 s) checks the flag OR mtime change. Hmm, over-engineering.

Let me settle on a clean design:

```
state:
  lastRunAt, lastStamp (string), running, queued

tick(reason):
  if running: queued = true; return
  stamp = newestSessionStamp()
  if reason === 'interval' && stamp === lastStamp: return   // nothing new
  running = true
  try { run archivers for each workspace } finally { running = false; lastStamp = newestSessionStamp() }
  if queued { queued = false; setTimeout(() => tick('queued'), 0) }
```

Triggers:
- `setInterval(() => tick('interval'), intervalMs)` — default 15 s.
- `ctx.on('session/event', () => debounced(() => tick('event'), debounceMs))` — default debounce 1.5 s? Hmm, `session/event` fires a lot, and a trailing debounce of 1.5s means during a long turn it fires only when there's a 1.5 s gap. Tool calls have gaps > 1.5 s sometimes, so it may run the archiver mid-turn several times. That's fine (idempotent, cheap) but each run rewrites the full conversation markdown. During a long turn with many tools, maybe 10-30 extra archiver runs. Each run = node process spawn (~100 ms) + decode of the session zstd + render. Sessions can get large (this one is ~26KB scripts but session logs can be MBs). Hmm, decoding a 5 MB session log every 1.5 s during a turn is wasteful.

Better: debounce longer (e.g. 5 s) and rely on `agent/turn-stopping` for the precise end-of-turn trigger? Hmm, but turn-stopping may precede the final log flush.

Hmm. Let me reconsider: what actually matters?
1. The final assistant reply must be archived (the exact gap AGENTS.md describes).
2. Reasonably prompt (seconds, not minutes).
3. Cheap.

A 15 s poll alone satisfies all three (worst case 15 s delay). AGENTS.md's PS watcher used 10 s and it was considered fine. So: **poll-only** is sufficient! Simplicity wins.

But we can do better cheaply: poll every 5 s but only run the archiver when the session-log stamp changed. Polling the filesystem (a recursive directory listing of $DSH_HOME/sessions) every 5 s — how expensive? `Get-ChildItem -Recurse` in PS was used by the PS watcher every 10 s. In Node, a recursive readdir of the sessions tree could be big (hundreds of session dirs, each with a few files). Let's see how the DSH sessions dir is laid out. Probably `$DSH_HOME/sessions/<workspace-slug>/<session>/session.v3.jsonl.zstd`. Let me check the size/structure.

Actually — I can do much better: **listen to `session/event` and set a dirty flag**; then a fast poll (every 2 s) that checks the dirty flag and the session-file stamp. If dirty, run. That gives ~2-3 s latency after the last event, without running the archiver mid-turn repeatedly... no wait, the dirty flag stays set during the turn and each 2 s tick would run the archiver. Same problem.

OK — the cleanest latency/cheapness tradeoff: **dirty flag + quiet-period detection**. Run the archiver when (a) dirty was set and (b) no session/event has arrived for `quietMs` (e.g. 3 s), OR (c) a long safety interval elapsed (e.g. 60 s). Implemented as: on each `session/event`, record `lastEventAt = Date.now()` and set `dirty = true`. A poll every 1 s (cheap, no FS access) checks: `dirty && Date.now() - lastEventAt >= quietMs` → run. Plus: every `safetyMs` (60 s) run unconditionally if the FS stamp changed (catches edits outside session events, e.g. subagent sessions or a previous process's tail... though session events cover those too).

Hmm, but wait: if the GUI is idle (no sessions at all), the safety poll reading the FS every 60 s is negligible. 

Actually — hmm, I realize I should double check something: does `session/event` fire for the *final assistant message* before turn end? The final assistant message is appended as a durable `assistant/message` event → yes, and `session/event` is "Post-commit, fire-and-forget append feed". So it fires. But the *on-disk* JSONL write may lag. Node's file append for the persistence plugin is likely synchronous-ish (`appendFile` awaits?). Since it's post-commit, "commit" for jsonl persistence = appended to the file. Actually "Post-commit" means the event was committed to the session (which for jsonl persistence means written to the log). So by the time `session/event` fires, the on-disk data should be there. 

So a 3 s quiet period after the last event → archive. That's exactly "每轮对话后自动归档".

I'm now fairly confident about the design. But I want to double check one risk: does a root-context `ctx.on('session/event', ...)` listener actually receive events emitted inside a session scope? In cordis, `emit` walks up: listeners registered on the emitting context and its ancestors are called. Let me verify in the cordis source. Let me grep for `emit(` in cordis core.

Actually, I know koishi/cordis semantics: `ctx.emit` dispatches to listeners registered on that context and all its *parent* contexts. Yes, `Context.prototype.emit` uses `this[Context.filter]` / registry walking up the parent chain. Let me verify quickly to be safe, because if root listeners don't receive scoped events, the primary trigger fails and only the safety poll works (still functional, just slower). Let me check.

Let me look at cordis's emit implementation.

Also, alternatively I can listen with `ctx.on('session/created', ...)` to hook per-session `ctx.on('session/event')` in the session scope. That's more code. Let me first verify root-level listening.

### Plugin design (final)

**Directory**: `C:\Users\sfy\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch\`

**package.json**:
```json
{
  "name": "dsh-archive-watch",
  "description": "DSH 宿主插件：每轮对话结束后自动把会话日志归档到工作区 prompt/（替代手动启动的 archive-watch.ps1）。",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "type": "module",
  "engines": { "node": "^22.19.0 || >=24.0.0" },
  "main": "lib/index.js",
  "exports": { ".": "./lib/index.js", "./package.json": "./package.json" },
  "dsh": { "bundle": { "patch": "./cordis.patch.yml" } },
  "files": ["lib/**/*.js", "cordis.patch.yml", "README.md"],
  "keywords": ["deepseek-harness", "dsh", "dsh-plugin", "archive", "session-log"],
  "private": true,
  "license": "MIT"
}
```
Note: dsh-pdf-pro has `"engines"` — copy that shape.

**cordis.patch.yml**:
```yaml
# dsh-archive-watch bundle patch：把「自动归档」宿主插件插入 web profile 的插件清单。
# 安装：
#   dsh plugin --profile web add C:\Users\sfy\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch
# 安装后该包自动进入 profile package.json 的 dsh.profile.bundles，重启 GUI 即生效。
- insert:
    - id: archive-watch
      name: 'dsh-archive-watch'
      config:
        workspaces:
          - E:/testPlatform
        quietSeconds: 3
        pollSeconds: 5
        safetySeconds: 60
        bootDelaySeconds: 3
```

Hmm: `pollSeconds` — in my design, the "poll" is an in-memory tick that checks the dirty flag/quiet period; the safety interval does the FS check. Let me finalize the algorithm cleanly:

```
lastEventAt = 0        // 最近一次会话事件时间
dirty = false          // 有事件尚未归档
lastRunAt = 0
lastStamp = ''         // 最近一次运行时看到的会话日志时间戳集合
running = false
queued = false

onSessionEvent():
  lastEventAt = Date.now(); dirty = true

tick():   // 每 1s 一次轻量 tick（纯内存判断）
  now = Date.now()
  if dirty && now - lastEventAt >= quietMs: run('event')
  else if now - lastRunAt >= safetyMs: run('safety')

run(reason):
  if running: queued = true; return
  running = true
  try:
     stamp = newestStamp()
     if reason === 'safety' && stamp === lastStamp: return   // 兜底轮询没变化就不跑
     ... run archiver for each workspace ...
     lastStamp = stamp
     lastRunAt = now
     dirty = false
  finally:
     running = false
     if queued { queued = false; scheduleTickSoon() }
```

Hmm, the tick at 1 s is a trivial in-memory check. Fine. Actually let me simplify: a single interval of 1 s (tickIntervalMs, default 1) doing the above. The FS scan (newestStamp) happens only when we're about to run the archiver (twice: before/after). The recursive FS scan of `$DSH_HOME/sessions` — how big is that tree? Let me check.

Actually — simpler: **let the archiver itself be the cheap check**. The archiver, when nothing changed, just stats each session file and rewrites INDEX.md + state.json. That's ~10 file stats + 2 small writes. Honestly that IS cheap (a few ms). The node process spawn is the main cost (~50-150 ms on Windows). Running that every 60 s at idle = negligible.

So: drop the FS scan entirely? Then at idle we spawn node every 60 s. Over a 12 h session: 720 spawns. Negligible CPU, but each run rewrites INDEX.md + .archive-state.json (720 × 2 small writes). Acceptable.

But with the FS stamp check, we avoid it entirely when idle. The FS scan cost: `readdir` recursive over `$DSH_HOME/sessions`. Let me measure the tree size. If it's, say, 200 files, a recursive readdir every 60 s is nothing. Let me just check.

Let me measure: count files under $DSH_HOME/sessions.

**runArchiver(ws)**:
```js
function runArchiver(ws, reason) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let child
    try {
      child = spawn(process.execPath, [archiverPath, '--workspace', ws], {
        cwd: ws, windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'], env: process.env,
      })
    } catch (e) { logLine(ws, `归档启动失败：${e.message}`); return resolve() }
    let out = '', err = ''
    child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { out += d; if (out.length > 65536) out = out.slice(-65536) })
    child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { err += d; if (err.length > 65536) err = err.slice(-65536) })
    const timer = setTimeout(() => { try { child.kill() } catch {} }, maxMs)
    child.on('error', (e) => { clearTimeout(timer); logLine(ws, `归档进程错误：${e.message}`); resolve() })
    child.on('close', (code) => {
      clearTimeout(timer)
      ...log...
      resolve()
    })
    children.add(child)  // for dispose
  })
}
```
Track running children in a Set so `ctx.effect` disposal can kill them.

Log format (keep the existing PS format so the existing AGENTS.md check keeps working):
```
[2026-09-21 20:05:12] 已归档（触发：回合结束，更新 2 个会话，耗时 412ms）
[2026-09-21 20:05:12] [warn] ...
```

Note: the archiver's stdout on success contains `[archive] 本次更新 2 个会话，跳过 1 个` or `[archive] 全部最新，无需更新（跳过 3 个）`. I'll parse:
- `本次更新 (\d+) 个会话` → n>0 → log the line
- else → no log (silent)
- stderr non-empty → log `[warn] <first line>`.

Also log at mount (boot): `archive-watch 插件已挂载：工作区 E:\testPlatform（安静 3s / 兜底 60s）`.

**Config**:
```js
const DEFAULT = { workspaces: [], quietSeconds: 3, tickSeconds: 1, safetySeconds: 60, bootDelaySeconds: 3, maxArchiveSeconds: 120, logFile: 'prompt/.archive-watch.log', archiver: 'code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs' }
```

Wait — `logFile` relative to workspace; and `archiver` relative to workspace. Good, makes it generic.

Hmm, but a hardcoded 'code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs' assumes the workspace has that layout. Configurable. Good.

**Write the log**: use `appendFileSync` and rotate at 512KB like the PS script (keep last 500 lines). Rotation needs reading the file; do it only when size > 512KB (rare).

**workspace path normalization**: On Windows, `E:/testPlatform` → archiver resolves `resolve('E:/testPlatform')` fine. For the log path: `join(ws, logFile)` → `E:/testPlatform/prompt/.archive-watch.log`. Node handles forward slashes on Windows. Good.

**Important**: `--workspace` must match the session header's cwd exactly (after normalize: lowercase, strip trailing slashes). The session cwd is probably `E:\testPlatform`. `resolve('E:/testPlatform')` → `E:\testPlatform`. The archiver normalizes both sides. Good.

**dispose**: 
```js
ctx.effect(() => {
  const timer = setInterval(tick, tickMs)
  const off = ctx.on('session/event', onEvent)
  return () => { clearInterval(timer); off(); for (const c of children) try{c.kill()}catch{} }
}, 'archive-watch: watch loop')
```

- `logFile`: 'prompt/.archive-watch.log'
- `quietSeconds`: 3 — 事件停止后多久触发归档
- `tickSeconds`: 1 — 内存检查频率
- `safetySeconds`: 60 — 兜底轮询（检查会话日志时间戳）
- `bootSeconds`: 3 — 启动后首次归档延迟
- `maxArchiveSeconds`: 180 — 单次归档进程超时

Algorithm (final):
```
let lastEventAt = 0, dirty = false, lastRunAt = 0, running = false, queued = false
let stamp = ''  // 最近一次扫描到的会话日志指纹

tick():
  if (running) return
  now = Date.now()
  if (dirty && now - lastEventAt >= quietMs) return run('event')
  if (now - lastRunAt >= safetyMs) return run('safety')

run(reason):
  if (running) { queued = true; return }
  running = true
  const next = scanStamp()
  if (reason === 'safety' && next === lastStamp) { running = false; lastRunAt = Date.now(); return }
  ... await archive all workspaces ...
  lastStamp = scanStamp()
  lastRunAt = Date.now()
  if (reason === 'event') dirty = false
  running = false
  if (queued) { queued = false; setTimeout(() => tick(), 0) }
```

Hmm — a subtlety: after an 'event'-triggered run, `dirty=false`. If new events arrived during the run, `onEvent` sets dirty=true again. Fine.

Another subtlety: the 'event' run happens `quietMs` after the *last* event — good.

But careful: `tick()` runs every 1 s and each call to `run('event')` spawns a node process per workspace. If the agent is idle after a turn, one run. Good. But during a long turn with tool calls, events keep flowing with <3s gaps mostly... but not always (a 30 s tool call → 3 s after the tool starts, dirty is set and no new events arrive → run!). ```js
/**
 * dsh-archive-watch — DSH 宿主插件：每轮对话结束后自动归档会话日志。
 * ...
 */
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process'
import { appendFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { homedir } from 'node:os'
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path'

export const name = 'archive-watch'

const DEFAULTS = {
  workspaces: [],
  archiver: 'code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs',
  logFile: 'prompt/.archive-watch.log',
  quietSeconds: 3,
  tickSeconds: 1,
  safetySeconds: 60,
  minSeconds: 5,
  bootSeconds: 3,
  maxArchiveSeconds: 180,
  logMaxBytes: 512 * 1024,
}
```

`scanStamp()`:
```js
function sessionsRoot() {
  const home = process.env.DSH_HOME || join(homedir(), '.dsh')
  return join(home, 'sessions')
}
function scanStamp(root) {
  let latest = 0, count = 0
  const walk = (dir, depth) => {
    if (depth > 4) return
    let entries
    try { entries = readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }) } catch { return }
    for (const entry of entries) {
      const full = join(dir, entry.name)
      if (entry.isDirectory()) walk(full, depth + 1)
      else if (entry.isFile()) { count++; try { const s = statSync(full); if (s.mtimeMs > latest) latest = s.mtimeMs } catch {} }
    }
  }
  walk(root, 0)
  return `${count}:${Math.round(latest)}`
}
```

Hmm, `withFileTypes` + statSync per file — 28 files, fine.

Then:

```js
export function apply(ctx, config) {
  try {
    applyInner(ctx, config ?? {})
  } catch (error) {
    try { ctx.logger?.warn?.(`archive-watch: 挂载失败：${String(error?.stack ?? error)}`) } catch {}
  }
}

function applyInner(ctx, rawConfig) {
  const cfg = { ...DEFAULTS, ...rawConfig }
  const targets = (Array.isArray(cfg.workspaces) ? cfg.workspaces : [cfg.workspaces])
    .filter((w) => typeof w === 'string' && w.trim())
    .map((w) => resolve(w))
  if (!targets.length) {
    ctx.logger?.warn?.('archive-watch: 未配置 workspaces，插件不做事')
    return
  }
  const quietMs = Math.max(0, cfg.quietSeconds * 1000)
  ...
}
```

I need to be careful: `ctx.logger?.warn?.()` — optional chaining is fine in modern Node ESM.

Logging function:
```js
function logLine(target, message) {
  try {
    const file = join(target, cfg.logFile)
    mkdirSync(dirname(file), { recursive: true })
    const stamp = `[${formatLocal(new Date())}] `
    appendFileSync(file, stamp + message + '\n', 'utf8')
    rotate(file)
  } catch {}
}
```
`formatLocal`: `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss` local.

Run archiver:
```js
function archiveWorkspace(target) {
  return new Promise((done) => {
    const script = join(target, cfg.archiver)
    if (!existsSync(script)) { logLine(target, `跳过：找不到归档脚本 ${script}`); return done() }
    const started = Date.now()
    let child
    try {
      child = spawn(process.execPath, [script, '--workspace', target], {
        cwd: target, windowsHide: true, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'], env: process.env,
      })
    } catch (error) { logLine(target, `归档进程启动失败：${String(error?.message ?? error)}`); return done() }
    runningChildren.add(child)
    let stdout = '', stderr = ''
    const tail = (current, chunk) => { const next = current + String(chunk); return next.length > 65536 ? next.slice(-65536) : next }
    child.stdout?.on('data', (chunk) => { stdout = tail(stdout, chunk) })
    child.stderr?.on('data', (chunk) => { stderr = tail(stderr, chunk) })
    const killer = setTimeout(() => { try { child.kill() } catch {} }, cfg.maxArchiveSeconds * 1000)
    const finish = (note) => {
      clearTimeout(killer)
      runningChildren.delete(child)
      const ms = Date.now() - started
      const noise = stderr.trim()
      if (noise) logLine(target, `[warn] ${firstLine(noise)}`)
      const updated = /本次更新\s*(\d+)\s*个会话/.exec(stdout)
      if (updated && Number(updated[1]) > 0) logLine(target, `已归档（触发：${note}，更新 ${updated[1]} 个会话，耗时 ${ms}ms）`)
      else if (note === 'boot') logLine(target, `启动检查完成（无需更新，耗时 ${ms}ms）`)
      done()
    }
    child.on('error', (error) => { logLine(target, `归档进程错误：${String(error?.message ?? error)}`); clearTimeout(killer); runningChildren.delete(child); done() })
    child.on('close', () => finish(reasonLabel))
  })
}
```

Hmm, I need `reason` in scope. Let me restructure so `archiveWorkspace(target, reason)`.

Note: `child.kill()` on timeout → 'close' fires → finish logs. And the timeout message should be noted. Fine, keep simple.

Dispose: kill children.

Now `ctx.effect`:
```js
ctx.effect(() => {
  const tickTimer = setInterval(tick, tickMs)
  const bootTimer = setTimeout(() => { void run('boot') }, bootMs)
  const offEvent = ctx.on('session/event', onSessionEvent)
  return () => {
    clearInterval(tickTimer); clearTimeout(bootTimer)
    try { offEvent() } catch {}
    for (const child of runningChildren) { try { child.kill() } catch {} }
    runningChildren.clear()
  }
}, 'archive-watch: 归档循环')
```

Wait: `ctx.on` inside `ctx.effect` — the effect callback runs immediately upon registration (`ctx.effect(() => {...})` executes the callback and stores the returned disposer). Yes, in cordis `ctx.effect(callback)` calls `callback()` immediately. Confirmed by dsh-pdf-pro (`ctx.effect(() => { const disposers = tools.map(...); return () => ... })` — registration happens inside).

Hmm, but careful: in `ctx.effect`, if the fiber disposes, the returned disposer runs. Good.

Also `ctx.logger.info` at mount.

Now `run`:
```js
async function run(reason) {
  if (running) { queued = true; return }
  running = true
  try {
    const before = scanStamp()
    if (reason === 'safety' && before === lastStamp) { lastRunAt = Date.now(); return }
    for (const target of targets) await archiveWorkspace(target, reason)
    lastStamp = scanStamp()
    lastRunAt = Date.now()
    if (reason === 'event') dirty = false
  } catch (error) {
    ctx.logger?.warn?.(`archive-watch: 归档失败：${String(error?.stack ?? error)}`)
  } finally {
    running = false
    if (queued) { queued = false; setTimeout(() => { void run('queued') }, 0) }
  }
}
```
Hmm, `return` inside try with finally → fine.

Careful: `lastRunAt = Date.now()` in the early-return path — good (prevents tight looping).

`tick`:
```js
function tick() {
  if (running) return
  const now = Date.now()
  if (dirty && now - lastEventAt >= quietMs) { void run('event'); return }
  if (now - lastRunAt >= safetyMs && now - lastRunAt >= minMs) { void run('safety') }
}
```
Hmm, `minMs` should also gate the event path. Let me unify: `if (now - lastRunAt < minMs) return` at the top.

```js
function tick() {
  if (running) return
  const now = Date.now()
  if (now - lastRunAt < minMs) return
  if (dirty && now - lastEventAt >= quietMs) { void run('event'); return }
  if (now - lastRunAt >= safetyMs) void run('safety')
}
```

function onSessionEvent() { lastEventAt = Date.now(); dirty = true }
```
Signature is `(session, event)` — we ignore args. Fine.

Optimization: filter by cwd — `session.header.cwd` matches a configured workspace. That avoids pointless runs. Let me do it:
```js
function onSessionEvent(session) {
  try {
    const cwd = session?.header?.cwd
    if (typeof cwd === 'string' && !targets.some((t) => samePath(t, cwd))) return
  } catch {}
  lastEventAt = Date.now(); dirty = true
}
```

And the event signature is `(session: Session, event: SessionEvent)`. So `session.header.cwd` is fine. But per the "don't serialize live data" rule — reading a leaf field is fine. Wrap in try/catch anyway.

```js
function rotate(file) {
  try {
    if (statSync(file).size <= cfg.logMaxBytes) return
    const kept = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/).slice(-500).join('\n')
    writeFileSync(file, kept, 'utf8')
  } catch {}
}
```

async function archiveWorkspace(target, reason) {
  const script = join(target, archiver)
  if (!existsSync(script)) return { missing: true, ms: 0, count: 0, stderr: '' }
  for (const mode of MODES) {  // [{silent:false, stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe']}, {silent:true, stdio:['ignore','ignore','ignore']}]
    const result = await attempt(script, target, mode)
    if (result.ok) return { ...result, silent: mode.silent }
    if (mode !== MODES[MODES.length-1]) continue  // 换一种 stdio 再试
    return { ...result, silent: mode.silent, error: result.error }
  }
}
```

354: 
355:       const after = scanStamp(root)
356:       lastStamp = after
357:       lastRunAt = Date.now()
358:       if (reason === 'event') dirty = false

364:         }

# 作用：挂载 Claude Code 兼容的 hook 桥，让「每次对话结束」自动把会话日志归档到 prompt/。
# hook 定义见 .dsh/hooks.json（Stop + UserPromptSubmit → code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs）。
- name: '@deepseek-ai/dsh-hooks-claude-code'
  config:
    configPath: E:/testPlatform/.dsh/hooks.json
    projectDir: E:/testPlatform
    defaultTimeoutMs: 60000
```

Hmm, what about validating an actual boot? `dsh web --help` shows web flags. Booting a second server would collide on port 3080. I could use a different port: `dsh web --port 3099`. But starting a second DSH process would open the same session storage (jsonl append) — two processes writing session state could corrupt? Actually a second process would only write sessions it creates. It shares `$DSH_HOME/sessions` and `storages`. Risky-ish but probably fine. Still, the instructions discourage starting another server.

Better idea: **`dsh --profile headless "..."`**? That boots the full host composition (including my plugin!) and runs one task. That would be a REAL end-to-end validation of the plugin in the host process — and it proves the piped stdio path works. Cost: one small model request (tokens) and it creates a session. Hmm, "headless" profile — is it installed? `dsh --profile headless "run the tests"` is in the help examples. So `headless` isn't installed. `dsh --from-default-profile headless` would create it — that's a new profile, not the web one, so my plugin (installed in web) wouldn't be there. Not useful.

What about `dsh --profile web <args>`? The web app runs the server. No dump-and-exit mode.

Hmm, what about a **third option**: compose+activate validation without serving. Is there a `dsh` mode that boots and exits? Not really.

OK: I'll rely on (a) the dump-config composition check, (b) the offline plugin self-test, (c) the fact that the plugin can't throw (everything wrapped), and (d) the honest note that the pipe path is proven by dsh-subprocess-local. Plus I'll give the user a precise verification command after restart.

Hmm, one more consideration about boot: the plugin's `ctx.effect(() => {...}, label)` — if `ctx.on` throws inside the effect callback, `apply`'s try/catch won't catch it because `ctx.effect` invokes the callback synchronously → yes it would throw synchronously inside `start()` → caught by `apply`'s try/catch. Good.

Hmm, but if the effect callback throws *after* partially registering (the setInterval and setTimeout created before ctx.on throws), those timers would leak (the returned disposer never registers). Minor edge case; `ctx.on('session/event', ...)` won't throw in practice.

Let me now also double check one runtime risk: **`ctx.effect` with a label** — the second arg. dsh-pdf-pro uses `ctx.effect(fn, "label")`. Confirmed fine.

And `ctx.logger?.warn?.()` — `ctx.logger` exists in cordis contexts (LoggerService mixed in). Since `inject` is empty and the plugin activates at boot, `ctx.logger` should be available. If not, optional chaining protects us.

Current §3 text (from the system reminder):

```
## 3. 铁律：每轮对话都必须归档（必须执行，不允许省略）

`prompt/` 里要保存**完整**的对话过程（**尤其是用户发的消息和助手的回复**），所以每个回合都要把
DSH 会话日志同步一次：

1. **回合开始时**（收到用户消息、开始动手前）执行一次，把上一回合的尾巴补齐：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```
   并顺手确认常驻归档守护进程是否活着（它是补上"最后一条回复"的关键）：
   ```powershell
   Get-Content prompt\.archive-watch.log -Tail 3
   ```
   如果日志超过 1 分钟没有新记录、或文件不存在，用后台任务把它拉起来：
   ```powershell
   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -NoProfile -File code\tools\archive-watch.ps1 -IntervalSeconds 10 -MaxHours 12
   ```
2. **回合结束前**（给出最终答复之前，最后一个工具调用）再执行一次：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```

### 为什么必须要有守护进程（重要，别删）

助手的一条回复是在**本轮最后一次工具调用之后**才生成的，因此回合内运行的归档**永远抓不到最后那条回复**；
两个回合之间若没有任何东西在运行，用户就会看到"最后一句话之后的内容没归档"。
`code/tools/archive-watch.ps1` 每 10 秒检查一次会话日志，一变就归档，从根上补上这段空档
（DSH hook 也能做到，但需要重启 GUI 才生效；守护进程不需要）。
```

53: ### 为什么必须要有守护进程（重要，别删）
54: 
55: 助手的一条回复是在**本轮最后一次工具调用之后**才生成的，因此回合内运行的归档**永远抓不到最后那条回复**；
56: 两个回合之间若没有任何东西在运行，用户就会看到"最后一句话之后的内容没归档"。
57: `code/tools/archive-watch.ps1` 每 10 秒检查一次会话日志，一变就归档，从根上补上这段空档
58: （DSH hook 也能做到，但需要重启 GUI 才生效；守护进程不需要）。
59: 
60: 规则与边界：
61: 
62: - 这是**强制步骤**，不是"可选优化"。即使会话里只是聊天、没有改代码，也要执行。
63: - 归档数据源是 DSH 落盘的会话日志（`$DSH_HOME/sessions/.../session.v3.jsonl.zstd`），
64:   **不要**用手写总结代替它；也不要把对话内容手工粘贴进 `prompt/`。
65: - 该脚本零依赖、幂等、永远退出码 0：重复执行只重写有变化的会话。静默运行即可，不要把它
66:   的输出贴给用户（除非用户明确问起归档情况）。
67: - 若脚本报错，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs`（去掉 `--quiet`）复盘并把错误写进答复。
68: - 用户问"归档好了吗"时，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --list` 和 `prompt/INDEX.md` 回答。
69: - **归档必须包含「用户消息」与「助手回复」**：这两类内容在 `prompt/对话正文/` 里必须逐条可见。
70:   归档后如果发现用户消息缺失或为空，说明 DSH 事件结构变化了（例如 `user/message` 的内容位置），
71:   先去 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 修解析，再 `--force` 重新生成；不要用"少几条无所谓"糊弄过去。
72: - 归档有两类会话：**主对话**（你和用户的对话）与**子代理会话**（你派生的子任务）。
73:   文件名里带「子代理-」前缀的是后者；主对话永远是索引里第一张表。
74: - **绝对不要**把 `prompt/` 加进 `.gitignore`，也不要删除或"清理"里面的内容。
75: - 若本工作区启用了 DSH 的 hook 自动化（见 `.dsh/`），脚本会额外在每轮结束时被 DSH 自动调用；
76:   即便如此，上面的两步仍然照做（双保险，成本很低）。
77: - 用户中断/取消某轮时也要尽量补齐：下一回合开始的第 1 步就是补归档。
78: 
79: ## 4. 平台开发约定
80: 
81: - 语言：与用户交流、界面文案、注释、文档统一用**简体中文**。
82: - 技术栈：以"能立刻跑起来"为第一原则，优先 Node.js 内置能力，新增依赖必须写进 `code/` 下的清单
83:   并在 `readme.md` 里说明安装步骤。
84: - 平台必须能**自证可用**：`code/` 里要带示例被测对象（如示例 Web 服务）与示例用例，
85:   让任何人克隆后一条命令即可跑通全流程。
86: - 每次交付前自测通过，并把关键结论（运行命令、结果、截图/录像路径）写进 `readme.md`。
87: - 需要用户决策的（技术栈大改、装大依赖、动全局配置）先问，不要擅自替用户决定。
88: 
89: ## 5. 录像约定（video/）
90: 
91: - 只有**真实跑通**的测试过程才录，命名：`video/YYYYMMDD-<被测对象>-<场景>.mp4`。
92: - 每个录像配一个同名 `.md`，写明：被测软件、测试范围、运行命令、结论、时长。
93: - 录屏方案在 `readme.md` 里说明；若本机缺少录屏工具，先与用户确认方案再动手。
94: 
95: ## 6. 环境事实（本机）
96: 
97: - 工作区：`E:\testPlatform`（Windows，PowerShell 5.1，非 pwsh 7）。
98: - 已装：Node.js v24.16.0、npm 11.13.0、Python 3.9.13、Git 2.54、DSH（`DSH_HOME=C:\Users\sfy\.dsh`）。
99: - **沙箱限制**：不要把命令输出管道给 `Select-Object`/`Out-String` 等（会触发命名管道拒绝）；

助手的一条回复是在**本轮最后一次工具调用之后**才生成的，因此回合内运行的归档**永远抓不到最后那条回复**；

`C:\Users\sfy\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch`，装在 web profile 的 `dsh.profile.bundles` 里）：

随 GUI 启动自动生效，**不需要每次重启 GUI 手动启动脚本**。

`code/tools/archive-watch.ps1` 是同一件事的外部常驻进程版本，现在只作为应急后备：
插件失效（例如 profile 被重装、GUI 没重启过）时手动拉起来用。

<system-reminder>
Updated instructions from: AGENTS.md

This file changed after it was loaded. Use the following content instead of the previously loaded instructions from this file.

# AGENTS.md — 软件测试平台（testPlatform）

本文件由 DSH 在**每个新会话**自动加载。任何模型/助手在本工作区工作时，都必须遵守这里的约定。

## 1. 项目目标

在 `E:\testPlatform` 里构建一个**通用软件测试平台**：能对任意被测软件（Web 应用、HTTP 接口、
命令行程序等）定义并执行测试用例、采集结果、生成报告，并把成功运行的演示过程录制下来。

## 2. 目录结构与职责

| 路径 | 职责 | 谁维护 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `readme.md` | 平台使用说明（安装、启动、编写用例、查看报告、录屏位置） | 助手维护，随功能更新 |
| `code/` | 平台全部代码（源码、脚本、示例用例、依赖清单） | 助手维护 |
| `video/` | 平台成功运行、对真实软件完成测试的录像（含录像说明） | 助手录制并登记 |
| `prompt/` | 与本项目相关的**完整人机对话记录**（自动归档，来源是 DSH 会话日志） | 只能由 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 写入 |
| `AGENTS.md` | 本文件：工作区铁律 | 助手维护 |
| `.dsh/` | DSH 自动化配置（hook 定义、profile 补丁） | 助手维护 |

### prompt/ 内部结构（自动生成，禁止手工编辑）

```
prompt/INDEX.md                  会话索引（主对话置顶，一眼可见）
prompt/对话正文/<时间>-<标题>.md    ★只有「用户消息」+「助手回复正文」，最常看的就是这个
prompt/会话全文/<时间>-<标题>.md    完整过程（含工具调用、思考、命令输出，长内容有截断提示）
prompt/原始事件/<会话ID>.jsonl      解码后的原始事件流（完全保真）
prompt/.archive-state.json       增量归档状态
```

## 3. 铁律：每轮对话都必须归档（必须执行，不允许省略）

`prompt/` 里要保存**完整**的对话过程（**尤其是用户发的消息和助手的回复**），所以每个回合都要把
DSH 会话日志同步一次：

1. **回合开始时**（收到用户消息、开始动手前）执行一次，把上一回合的尾巴补齐：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```
   并顺手确认常驻归档机制是否活着（它是补上"最后一条回复"的关键）：
   ```powershell
   Get-Content prompt\.archive-watch.log -Tail 3
   ```
   **正常情况下什么都不用做**：`dsh-archive-watch` 宿主插件随 GUI 启动自动运行，
   每轮说完话约 3 秒就会自己归档，日志会不断出现「已归档（触发：event…）」。
   只有当日志超过 1 分钟没有新记录、或文件不存在时（说明插件没生效），
   才用后台任务把老的外部守护脚本当应急方案拉起来：
   ```powershell
   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -NoProfile -File code\tools\archive-watch.ps1 -IntervalSeconds 10 -MaxHours 12
   ```
   同时要在答复里告诉用户插件没生效（通常是 GUI 还没用新 profile 重启过），别默默降级。
2. **回合结束前**（给出最终答复之前，最后一个工具调用）再执行一次：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```

### 为什么必须要有常驻机制（重要，别删）

助手的一条回复是在**本轮最后一次工具调用之后**才生成的，因此回合内运行的归档**永远抓不到最后那条回复**；
两个回合之间若没有任何东西在运行，用户就会看到"最后一句话之后的内容没归档"。

现在这一环由**宿主插件**承担：`dsh-archive-watch`
（源码 `C:\Users\sfy\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch`，作为 bundle 装在 web profile 的
`dsh.profile.bundles` 里）。它监听宿主的 `session/event`，事件静默 3 秒即归档，
另有 60 秒兜底轮询；**随 GUI 启动自动生效，不需要每次重启 GUI 手动启动脚本**。

`code/tools/archive-watch.ps1` 是同一件事的"外部常驻进程"版本，现在只作为应急后备：
插件失效时手动拉起来用（插件与它都归档同一批会话，不会互相破坏，但会做重复功）。

规则与边界：

- 这是**强制步骤**，不是"可选优化"。即使会话里只是聊天、没有改代码，也要执行。
- 归档数据源是 DSH 落盘的会话日志（`$DSH_HOME/sessions/.../session.v3.jsonl.zstd`），
  **不要**用手写总结代替它；也不要把对话内容手工粘贴进 `prompt/`。
- 该脚本零依赖、幂等、永远退出码 0：重复执行只重写有变化的会话。静默运行即可，不要把它
  的输出贴给用户（除非用户明确问起归档情况）。
- 若脚本报错，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs`（去掉 `--quiet`）复盘并把错误写进答复。
- 用户问"归档好了吗"时，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --list` 和 `prompt/INDEX.md` 回答。
- **归档必须包含「用户消息」与「助手回复」**：这两类内容在 `prompt/对话正文/` 里必须逐条可见。
  归档后如果发现用户消息缺失或为空，说明 DSH 事件结构变化了（例如 `user/message` 的内容位置），
  先去 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 修解析，再 `--force` 重新生成；不要用"少几条无所谓"糊弄过去。
- 归档有两类会话：**主对话**（你和用户的对话）与**子代理会话**（你派生的子任务）。
  文件名里带「子代理-」前缀的是后者；主对话永远是索引里第一张表。
- **绝对不要**把 `prompt/` 加进 `.gitignore`，也不要删除或"清理"里面的内容。
- 若本工作区启用了 DSH 的 hook 自动化（见 `.dsh/`），脚本会额外在每轮结束时被 DSH 自动调用；
  即便如此，上面的两步仍然照做（双保险，成本很低）。
- 用户中断/取消某轮时也要尽量补齐：下一回合开始的第 1 步就是补归档。

## 4. 平台开发约定

- 语言：与用户交流、界面文案、注释、文档统一用**简体中文**。
- 技术栈：以"能立刻跑起来"为第一原则，优先 Node.js 内置能力，新增依赖必须写进 `code/` 下的清单
  并在 `readme.md` 里说明安装步骤。
- 平台必须能**自证可用**：`code/` 里要带示例被测对象（如示例 Web 服务）与示例用例，
  让任何人克隆后一条命令即可跑通全流程。
- 每次交付前自测通过，并把关键结论（运行命令、结果、截图/录像路径）写进 `readme.md`。
- 需要用户决策的（技术栈大改、装大依赖、动全局配置）先问，不要擅自替用户决定。

## 5. 录像约定（video/）

- 只有**真实跑通**的测试过程才录，命名：`video/YYYYMMDD-<被测对象>-<场景>.mp4`。
- 每个录像配一个同名 `.md`，写明：被测软件、测试范围、运行命令、结论、时长。
- 录屏方案在 `readme.md` 里说明；若本机缺少录屏工具，先与用户确认方案再动手。

## 6. 环境事实（本机）

- 工作区：`E:\testPlatform`（Windows，PowerShell 5.1，非 pwsh 7）。
- 已装：Node.js v24.16.0、npm 11.13.0、Python 3.9.13、Git 2.54、DSH（`DSH_HOME=C:\Users\sfy\.dsh`）。
- **沙箱限制**：不要把命令输出管道给 `Select-Object`/`Out-String` 等（会触发命名管道拒绝）；
  直接把输出写文件再读，或让输出原样打印。写工作区外的文件需要用户批准。

<system-reminder>
Updated instructions from: AGENTS.md

This file changed after it was loaded. Use the following content instead of the previously loaded instructions from this file.

# AGENTS.md — 软件测试平台（testPlatform）

本文件由 DSH 在**每个新会话**自动加载。任何模型/助手在本工作区工作时，都必须遵守这里的约定。

## 1. 项目目标

在 `E:\testPlatform` 里构建一个**通用软件测试平台**：能对任意被测软件（Web 应用、HTTP 接口、
命令行程序等）定义并执行测试用例、采集结果、生成报告，并把成功运行的演示过程录制下来。

## 2. 目录结构与职责

| 路径 | 职责 | 谁维护 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `readme.md` | 平台使用说明（安装、启动、编写用例、查看报告、录屏位置） | 助手维护，随功能更新 |
| `code/` | 平台全部代码（源码、脚本、示例用例、依赖清单） | 助手维护 |
| `video/` | 平台成功运行、对真实软件完成测试的录像（含录像说明） | 助手录制并登记 |
| `prompt/` | 与本项目相关的**完整人机对话记录**（自动归档，来源是 DSH 会话日志） | 只能由 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 写入 |
| `AGENTS.md` | 本文件：工作区铁律 | 助手维护 |
| `.dsh/` | DSH 自动化配置（hook 定义、profile 补丁） | 助手维护 |
| `~\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch\` | **自动归档宿主插件**（工作区外：装在 web profile 里，随 GUI 启动生效） | 助手维护 |
| `~\.dsh\profiles\web\` | web profile 的插件清单与补丁层（工作区外） | 助手维护 |

### prompt/ 内部结构（自动生成，禁止手工编辑）

```
prompt/INDEX.md                  会话索引（主对话置顶，一眼可见）
prompt/对话正文/<时间>-<标题>.md    ★只有「用户消息」+「助手回复正文」，最常看的就是这个
prompt/会话全文/<时间>-<标题>.md    完整过程（含工具调用、思考、命令输出，长内容有截断提示）
prompt/原始事件/<会话ID>.jsonl      解码后的原始事件流（完全保真）
prompt/.archive-state.json       增量归档状态
```

## 3. 铁律：每轮对话都必须归档（必须执行，不允许省略）

`prompt/` 里要保存**完整**的对话过程（**尤其是用户发的消息和助手的回复**），所以每个回合都要把
DSH 会话日志同步一次：

1. **回合开始时**（收到用户消息、开始动手前）执行一次，把上一回合的尾巴补齐：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```
   并顺手确认常驻归档机制是否活着（它是补上"最后一条回复"的关键）：
   ```powershell
   Get-Content prompt\.archive-watch.log -Tail 3
   ```
   **正常情况下什么都不用做**：`dsh-archive-watch` 宿主插件随 GUI 启动自动运行，
   每轮说完话约 3 秒就会自己归档，日志会不断出现「已归档（触发：event…）」。
   只有当日志超过 1 分钟没有新记录、或文件不存在时（说明插件没生效），
   才用后台任务把老的外部守护脚本当应急方案拉起来：
   ```powershell
   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -NoProfile -File code\tools\archive-watch.ps1 -IntervalSeconds 10 -MaxHours 12
   ```
   同时要在答复里告诉用户插件没生效（通常是 GUI 还没用新 profile 重启过），别默默降级。
2. **回合结束前**（给出最终答复之前，最后一个工具调用）再执行一次：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```

### 为什么必须要有常驻机制（重要，别删）

助手的一条回复是在**本轮最后一次工具调用之后**才生成的，因此回合内运行的归档**永远抓不到最后那条回复**；
两个回合之间若没有任何东西在运行，用户就会看到"最后一句话之后的内容没归档"。

现在这一环由**宿主插件**承担：`dsh-archive-watch`
（源码 `C:\Users\sfy\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch`，作为 bundle 装在 web profile 的
`dsh.profile.bundles` 里）。它监听宿主的 `session/event`，事件静默 3 秒即归档，
另有 60 秒兜底轮询；**随 GUI 启动自动生效，不需要每次重启 GUI 手动启动脚本**。

`code/tools/archive-watch.ps1` 是同一件事的"外部常驻进程"版本，现在只作为应急后备：
插件失效时手动拉起来用（插件与它都归档同一批会话，不会互相破坏，但会做重复功）。

规则与边界：

- 这是**强制步骤**，不是"可选优化"。即使会话里只是聊天、没有改代码，也要执行。
- 归档数据源是 DSH 落盘的会话日志（`$DSH_HOME/sessions/.../session.v3.jsonl.zstd`），
  **不要**用手写总结代替它；也不要把对话内容手工粘贴进 `prompt/`。
- 该脚本零依赖、幂等、永远退出码 0：重复执行只重写有变化的会话。静默运行即可，不要把它
  的输出贴给用户（除非用户明确问起归档情况）。
- 若脚本报错，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs`（去掉 `--quiet`）复盘并把错误写进答复。
- 用户问"归档好了吗"时，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --list` 和 `prompt/INDEX.md` 回答。
- **归档必须包含「用户消息」与「助手回复」**：这两类内容在 `prompt/对话正文/` 里必须逐条可见。
  归档后如果发现用户消息缺失或为空，说明 DSH 事件结构变化了（例如 `user/message` 的内容位置），
  先去 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 修解析，再 `--force` 重新生成；不要用"少几条无所谓"糊弄过去。
- 归档有两类会话：**主对话**（你和用户的对话）与**子代理会话**（你派生的子任务）。
  文件名里带「子代理-」前缀的是后者；主对话永远是索引里第一张表。
- **绝对不要**把 `prompt/` 加进 `.gitignore`，也不要删除或"清理"里面的内容。
- 若本工作区启用了 DSH 的 hook 自动化（见 `.dsh/`），脚本会额外在每轮结束时被 DSH 自动调用；
  即便如此，上面的两步仍然照做（双保险，成本很低）。
- 用户中断/取消某轮时也要尽量补齐：下一回合开始的第 1 步就是补归档。

## 4. 平台开发约定

- 语言：与用户交流、界面文案、注释、文档统一用**简体中文**。
- 技术栈：以"能立刻跑起来"为第一原则，优先 Node.js 内置能力，新增依赖必须写进 `code/` 下的清单
  并在 `readme.md` 里说明安装步骤。
- 平台必须能**自证可用**：`code/` 里要带示例被测对象（如示例 Web 服务）与示例用例，
  让任何人克隆后一条命令即可跑通全流程。
- 每次交付前自测通过，并把关键结论（运行命令、结果、截图/录像路径）写进 `readme.md`。
- 需要用户决策的（技术栈大改、装大依赖、动全局配置）先问，不要擅自替用户决定。

## 5. 录像约定（video/）

- 只有**真实跑通**的测试过程才录，命名：`video/YYYYMMDD-<被测对象>-<场景>.mp4`。
- 每个录像配一个同名 `.md`，写明：被测软件、测试范围、运行命令、结论、时长。
- 录屏方案在 `readme.md` 里说明；若本机缺少录屏工具，先与用户确认方案再动手。

## 6. 环境事实（本机）

- 工作区：`E:\testPlatform`（Windows，PowerShell 5.1，非 pwsh 7）。
- 已装：Node.js v24.16.0、npm 11.13.0、Python 3.9.13、Git 2.54、DSH（`DSH_HOME=C:\Users\sfy\.dsh`）。
- **沙箱限制**：不要把命令输出管道给 `Select-Object`/`Out-String` 等（会触

... [context truncated: 120 chars omitted, 完整内容见 prompt/原始事件/*.jsonl] ...

<system-reminder>
Updated instructions from: AGENTS.md

This file changed after it was loaded. Use the following content instead of the previously loaded instructions from this file.

# AGENTS.md — 软件测试平台（testPlatform）

本文件由 DSH 在**每个新会话**自动加载。任何模型/助手在本工作区工作时，都必须遵守这里的约定。

## 1. 项目目标

在 `E:\testPlatform` 里构建一个**通用软件测试平台**：能对任意被测软件（Web 应用、HTTP 接口、
命令行程序等）定义并执行测试用例、采集结果、生成报告，并把成功运行的演示过程录制下来。

## 2. 目录结构与职责

| 路径 | 职责 | 谁维护 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `readme.md` | 平台使用说明（安装、启动、编写用例、查看报告、录屏位置） | 助手维护，随功能更新 |
| `code/` | 平台全部代码（源码、脚本、示例用例、依赖清单） | 助手维护 |
| `video/` | 平台成功运行、对真实软件完成测试的录像（含录像说明） | 助手录制并登记 |
| `prompt/` | 与本项目相关的**完整人机对话记录**（自动归档，来源是 DSH 会话日志） | 只能由 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 写入 |
| `AGENTS.md` | 本文件：工作区铁律 | 助手维护 |
| `.dsh/` | DSH 自动化配置（hook 定义、profile 补丁） | 助手维护 |
| `~\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch\` | **自动归档宿主插件**（工作区外：装在 web profile 里，随 GUI 启动生效） | 助手维护 |
| `~\.dsh\profiles\web\` | web profile 的插件清单与补丁层（工作区外） | 助手维护 |

### prompt/ 内部结构（自动生成，禁止手工编辑）

```
prompt/INDEX.md                  会话索引（主对话置顶，一眼可见）
prompt/对话正文/<时间>-<标题>.md    ★只有「用户消息」+「助手回复正文」，最常看的就是这个
prompt/会话全文/<时间>-<标题>.md    完整过程（含工具调用、思考、命令输出，长内容有截断提示）
prompt/原始事件/<会话ID>.jsonl      解码后的原始事件流（完全保真）
prompt/.archive-state.json       增量归档状态
```

## 3. 铁律：每轮对话都必须归档（必须执行，不允许省略）

`prompt/` 里要保存**完整**的对话过程（**尤其是用户发的消息和助手的回复**），所以每个回合都要把
DSH 会话日志同步一次：

1. **回合开始时**（收到用户消息、开始动手前）执行一次，把上一回合的尾巴补齐：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```
   并顺手确认常驻归档机制是否活着（它是补上"最后一条回复"的关键）：
   ```powershell
   Get-Content prompt\.archive-watch.log -Tail 3
   ```
   **正常情况下什么都不用做**：`dsh-archive-watch` 宿主插件随 GUI 启动自动运行，
   每轮说完话约 3 秒就会自己归档，日志会不断出现「已归档（触发：event…）」。
   只有当日志超过 1 分钟没有新记录、或文件不存在时（说明插件没生效），
   才用后台任务把老的外部守护脚本当应急方案拉起来：
   ```powershell
   powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -NoProfile -File code\tools\archive-watch.ps1 -IntervalSeconds 10 -MaxHours 12
   ```
   同时要在答复里告诉用户插件没生效（通常是 GUI 还没用新 profile 重启过），别默默降级。
2. **回合结束前**（给出最终答复之前，最后一个工具调用）再执行一次：
   ```powershell
   node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --quiet
   ```

### 为什么必须要有常驻机制（重要，别删）

助手的一条回复是在**本轮最后一次工具调用之后**才生成的，因此回合内运行的归档**永远抓不到最后那条回复**；
两个回合之间若没有任何东西在运行，用户就会看到"最后一句话之后的内容没归档"。

现在这一环由**宿主插件**承担：`dsh-archive-watch`
（源码 `C:\Users\sfy\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch`，作为 bundle 装在 web profile 的
`dsh.profile.bundles` 里）。它监听宿主的 `session/event`，事件静默 3 秒即归档，
另有 60 秒兜底轮询；**随 GUI 启动自动生效，不需要每次重启 GUI 手动启动脚本**。

`code/tools/archive-watch.ps1` 是同一件事的"外部常驻进程"版本，现在只作为应急后备：
插件失效时手动拉起来用（插件与它都归档同一批会话，不会互相破坏，但会做重复功）。

规则与边界：

- 这是**强制步骤**，不是"可选优化"。即使会话里只是聊天、没有改代码，也要执行。
- 归档数据源是 DSH 落盘的会话日志（`$DSH_HOME/sessions/.../session.v3.jsonl.zstd`），
  **不要**用手写总结代替它；也不要把对话内容手工粘贴进 `prompt/`。
- 该脚本零依赖、幂等、永远退出码 0：重复执行只重写有变化的会话。静默运行即可，不要把它
  的输出贴给用户（除非用户明确问起归档情况）。
- 若脚本报错，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs`（去掉 `--quiet`）复盘并把错误写进答复。
- 用户问"归档好了吗"时，用 `node code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --list` 和 `prompt/INDEX.md` 回答。
- **归档必须包含「用户消息」与「助手回复」**：这两类内容在 `prompt/对话正文/` 里必须逐条可见。
  归档后如果发现用户消息缺失或为空，说明 DSH 事件结构变化了（例如 `user/message` 的内容位置），
  先去 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 修解析，再 `--force` 重新生成；不要用"少几条无所谓"糊弄过去。
- 归档有两类会话：**主对话**（你和用户的对话）与**子代理会话**（你派生的子任务）。
  文件名里带「子代理-」前缀的是后者；主对话永远是索引里第一张表。
- **绝对不要**把 `prompt/` 加进 `.gitignore`，也不要删除或"清理"里面的内容。
- hook 自动化（见 `.dsh/`）是**可选的双保险**：用 `dsh web --patch .dsh/profile-patch.yml` 启动时，
  UserPromptSubmit / Stop 会各调用一次归档脚本。写法上必须注意：`- name: xxx` 这种**没有 id、
  也不是 `insert` 的补丁行会被 loader 整个拒收**（报 `patch: id is required for non-insert patches`），
  正确写法是 `- insert:` 列表并给行一个 id（本工作区历史上就踩过这个坑，导致 hook 从未生效）。
- 用户中断/取消某轮时也要尽量补齐：下一回合开始的第 1 步就是补归档。

## 4. 平台开发约定

- 语言：与用户交流、界面文案、注释、文档统一用**简体中文**。
- 技术栈：以"能立刻跑起来"为第一原则，优先 Node.js 内置能力，新增依赖必须写进 `code/` 下的清单
  并在 `readme.md` 里说明安装步骤。
- 平台必须能**自证可用**：`code/` 里要带示例被测对象（如示例 Web 服务）与示例用例，
  让任何人克隆后一条命令即可跑通全流程。
- 每次交付前自测通过，并把关键结论（运行命令、结果、截图/录像路径）写进 `readme.md`。
- 需要用户决策的（技术栈大改、装大依赖、动全局配置）先问，不要擅自替用户决定。

## 5. 录像约定（video/）

- 只有**真实跑通**的测试过程才录，命名：`video/YYYYMMDD-<被测对象>-<场景>.mp4`。
- 每个录像配一个同名 `.md`，写明：被测软件、测试范围、运行命令、结论、时长。
- 录屏方案在 `readme.md` 里说明；若本机缺少录屏工具，先与用户确认方案再动手。

## 6. 环境事实（

### 🤖 助手

已固化完成。归档能力现在是一行**宿主插件**，随 GUI 启动自动生效，不再需要手动跑脚本。

## 做了什么

**新插件 `dsh-archive-watch`**（零依赖，只用 Node 内置模块），装在 `C:\Users\sfy\.dsh\plugins\dsh-archive-watch`，并作为 bundle 进了 web profile 的 `dsh.profile.bundles`：

- **主触发器**：监听宿主的 `session/event`（提交后的追加事件流）。一轮说完话、事件静默 3 秒即归档 —— 这正好补上「最后一条回复落在最后一次工具调用之后」的空档。
- **兜底轮询**：每 60 秒检查会话日志指纹（文件数 + 最新 mtime），事件监听失效也不漏。
- 只对 `workspaces` 里配置的工作区（当前 `E:/testPlatform`）生效；无关会话的事件被忽略。
- 所有副作用（定时器、事件监听、子进程、日志）都挂在 `ctx.effect` 上，卸载即全部回收。
- 插件**不重写归档逻辑**，只是按需跑 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs --workspace <ws>`；日志仍写 `prompt/.archive-watch.log`，格式与原来一致，所以 AGENTS.md 里那条检查命令继续有效。
- 为什么放宿主平面而不是 preset：守护必须全进程一份、跨会话共享，放进 preset 会变成每个会话各起一份互相争抢写入。

**顺手修掉一个既有 bug**：`.dsh/` 里那套 hook 从来没挂载过。profile 的 `cordis.patch.yml` 写的是 `- name: '@deepseek-ai/dsh-hooks-claude-code'`，既没有 `id` 也不是 `insert`，loader 直接拒收（`patch: id is required for non-insert patches`），每次启动只多打一行警告。现在全局那条死配置已清掉（改为注释指向插件），`.dsh/profile-patch.yml` 改成正确的 `- insert:` 形式 —— hook 现在是真正可用的可选双保险。

## 需要你做一步

**重启一次 GUI**（关掉当前 `dsh web` 再用同样方式启动）。插件行只在 profile 组合时挂载，这是唯一无法在会话内代劳的一步。

重启后验证：

```powershell
Get-Content E:\testPlatform\prompt\.archive-watch.log -Tail 5
```

应看到 `archive-watch 插件已挂载：…`，之后每轮对话结束出现 `已归档（触发：event，更新 N 个会话，第 K 次，耗时 …ms）`。

另外：当前那个手动启动的 `archive-watch.ps1` 会随宿主进程结束（或 12 小时到期自行退出），插件会接管；两者同时跑也不会互相破坏，只是重复劳动。

## 验证到什么程度 / 没验证到什么

已核实：插件模块可导入且导出正确；假 ctx 离线自测通过（真跑起了归档脚本并重写 `prompt/INDEX.md`、无关工作区不触发、卸载后彻底安静）；`dsh --profile web --dump-config` 显示该行已正确进入组合、profile 补丁不再报错；已安装的那份与自测通过的那份逐项一致。

**没能在会话内验证的**：插件在真实宿主进程里的子进程管道路径。自测跑在沙箱里，`pipe` 的 `spawn` 会同步抛 `EPERM` —— 所以自测实际走的是我加的第三级降级（`inherit`）。宿主进程本身没有这个限制（`dsh-subprocess-local` 就是宿主用管道拉起子进程的，证据是工具输出能被我读到），正常路径应命中管道分支；万一不成立，降级链会保证归档照跑，只是日志标注 `stdio 降级`。若重启后日志一直没有 `已归档`，按 AGENTS.md 第 3 节用 `archive-watch.ps1` 应急并把日志发我。

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程（第 2 轮 / 第 5 步）</summary>

Now I understand the data:

**`source.kind` values on `user/message`:**
- `user` — the human's message (possibly with rpcId/clientTimeZone)
- `agent-instructions` — AGENTS.md injection
- `plugin` — system-prompt snapshots / tool-jobs notices / tool-goal notices
- `skill-catalog` — skill list
- `agent-message` with `form: 'relay'` and `senderSessionId` — **a message relayed from another agent session** (e.g. a subagent sending a message to its parent; or the parent's delegation prompt to the child?). 

Interesting! In the subagent sessions (`e8f452b9`, `6552d01e`), the *delegation prompt itself* has `source: {"kind":"user"}` — look at the first sample: `source={"kind":"user"}` with text "你是一个前端工程师。请为一个已经写好后端的..." — that's MY delegation prompt, but it's recorded with `kind: "user"`!

So in the subagent session, the delegation prompt appears as `source.kind === 'user'` — indistinguishable from a real human message. **That's exactly the user's complaint.** In `prompt/对话正文/2026-09-21_1918-子代理-你是一个前端工程师...md`, the delegation prompt is rendered as "👤 用户" — which is wrong; that message came from me (the main agent), not from the human.

And `agent-message` / `form: 'relay'` with `senderSessionId` — those are messages relayed between sessions (subagent → parent, i.e. the subagent's report delivered to me; or parent → child sends). In the main session `session-8ad633b3` there are `agent-message` entries with senderSessionId = the subagents → those are the subagent reports sent to me.

Also in subagent sessions there are `agent-message` entries with `senderSessionId = session-8ad633b3` (the parent) → messages the parent (me) sent TO the subagent (e.g. the original delegation prompt delivered via relay, or follow-up `send_message`).

Wait, in e8f452b9: `2 x source={"kind":"agent-message","form":"relay","senderSessionId":"session-8ad633b3..."}`. So 2 messages came from the parent session. And 1 `kind: "user"` (the delegation prompt as recorded from the subagent start).

Hmm, so for `6552d01e`: `1 x kind:"user"` (delegation prompt) and no agent-message. For `e8f452b9`: `1 x kind:"user"` + 2 × agent-message from parent.

So the discriminator for "message from the human" vs "message from an agent":
1. If the session is a subagent session (`header.delegationDepth > 0`), then a `kind: 'user'` message is the delegation prompt from the parent agent, NOT the human.
2. `kind: 'agent-message'` → always from another agent (with `senderSessionId`).
3. Other kinds (`agent-instructions`, `plugin`, `skill-catalog`, `goal`, `subagent-settled`) → system-injected.

Also, in the main session, is there a case where `kind: 'user'` is not the human? E.g. steering messages injected by a plugin use `source: PLUGIN_SOURCE = {kind:'plugin', ...}`. So in a main session, `kind:'user'` = human. Good.

Hmm, but careful: what about the main session receiving an `agent-message` from a subagent (the subagent's final report)? In the main session's 对话正文, those are currently *excluded* (because `sourceKind !== 'user'`). Hmm — but they might be worth showing as "子代理回报". The user's ask is specifically about distinguishing:
- 用户发的消息 (human)
- 助手对子代理发的消息 (assistant → subagent)

So the fix should cover at least:
- In subagent archives: label the delegation prompt as "🧑💻 主代理（助手）发给本子代理的任务" instead of "👤 用户".
- In main archives: keep "👤 用户" for human messages, and clearly label messages that came from subagents (relay) as "📨 子代理发来的消息".
- Also in the subagent archive, messages relayed from the parent (agent-message, senderSessionId = parent) should be labeled as "主代理发来的消息".

Let me also handle: the 子代理 files' own title/header should say clearly that this is a subagent conversation, whose "user" is the delegating agent.

Now, what does `renderConversation` currently do? Let me read lines 369-450.

Also `renderSession` (会话全文) at line 291: if `sourceKind === 'user'` → "### 👤 用户". Needs the same distinction. And the `agent-message` ones would currently fall into the `<details>📎 系统注入上下文（source: agent-message）` bucket — which is misleading; they're real messages from an agent, not system context.

Let me read the rest of the rendering code.

Let me read lines 309-470.

427: function decodeSession(file) {
428:   const { events, badLines, bytes } = readSessionEvents(file)

So I have everything needed:
- `header.origin === 'subagent'` / `header.delegationDepth > 0` → this session is a subagent; its "user" messages are delegation prompts from `header.parentSession`.
- `source.kind === 'agent-message'` with `form: 'relay'` and `senderSessionId` → a message from another agent session (either the parent delegating/steering, or a child reporting back).

Now let me design the fix.

**1. Classify every `user/message` into an explicit role.**

Add a function `messageOrigin(message, header)` returning a descriptor:
- `{ role: 'human', label: '👤 用户（本人）', note: '' }` — `source.kind === 'user'` AND the session is not a subagent.

- `{ role: 'agent', label: '📨 来自其它代理会话的消息（senderSessionId）' }` — `source.kind === 'agent-message'`.

const ROLE = {
  human:  { icon: '👤', title: '用户（本人）' },
  parent: { icon: '🧑💻', title: '主代理（助手）→ 子代理' },

Function:
```js
/**
 * 判断一条 user/message 到底是谁发的。
 * 关键坑：子代理会话里，主代理的委派提示 source.kind 也是 'user'，
 * 直接当成「用户消息」会把人机对话和代理间通信混为一谈。
 */
function messageOrigin(message, header) {
  const kind = sourceKind(message)
  if (kind === 'user') {
    if (isSubagentSession(header)) {
      const parent = header?.parentSession ? `（来自会话 ${header.parentSession}）` : ''
      return { role: 'parent', kind, label: `🧑💻 主代理（助手）→ 子代理：委派任务${parent}` }
    }
    return { role: 'human', kind, label: '👤 用户（本人）' }
  }
  if (kind === 'agent-message') {
    const sender = message?.source?.senderSessionId ?? '未知会话'
    const fromParent = sender === header?.parentSession
    const direction = isSubagentSession(header)
      ? (fromParent ? '主代理（助手）→ 本子代理' : `其它代理会话 ${sender}`)
      : `子代理/其它代理会话 ${sender}`
    return { role: 'agent', kind, label: `📨 代理间消息：${direction}` }
  }
  return { role: 'system', kind, label: `📎 系统注入上下文（source: ${kind}）` }
}
```

### Edit 1: helpers after `sourceKind`

Replace:
```js

Let me write the new block:

```js
/** 归一化“来源”，用于区分人类输入与系统注入的上下文。 */
function sourceKind(message) {
  const source = message?.source
  if (!source || typeof source !== 'object') return 'unknown'
  return source.kind ?? 'unknown'
}

/** 会话是不是「被主代理委派出来的子代理会话」。 */
function isSubagentSession(header) {
  return (header?.delegationDepth ?? 0) > 0 || header?.origin === 'subagent'
}

/**
 * 判断一条 `user/message` 到底是谁发的。
 *
 * 这里有个必须绕开的坑：**子代理会话里，主代理（助手）的委派提示 source.kind 也是 'user'**，
 * 如果只看 source.kind 就打成「用户消息」，人机对话和代理间通信就混在一起了 ——
 * 读归档的人会以为那句「你是一个前端工程师…」是自己发的。
 * 所以先看会话身份（子代理会话里的 user 必然是主代理派活），再看 source.kind。
 */
function messageOrigin(message, header) {
  const kind = sourceKind(message)
  if (kind === 'user') {
    if (isSubagentSession(header)) {
      const from = header?.parentSession ? `（来自会话 \`${header.parentSession}\`）` : ''
      return { role: 'parent', kind, label: `🧑💻 主代理（助手）→ 本子代理 · 委派任务${from}` }
    }
    return { role: 'human', kind, label: '👤 用户（本人）' }
  }
  if (kind === 'agent-message') return { role: 'agent', kind, label: agentRelayLabel(message, header) }
  return { role: 'system', kind, label: `📎 系统注入上下文（source: ${kind}）` }
}

/** 代理间中转消息的署名：谁发给谁。 */
function agentRelayLabel(message, header) {
  const sender = message?.source?.senderSessionId ?? '未知会话'
  const short = shortSessionId(sender)
  if (isSubagentSession(header)) {
    if (header?.parentSession && sender === header.parentSession) return `📨 主代理（助手）→ 本子代理 · 追加消息（来自 \`${short}\`）`
    return `📨 其它代理会话 → 本子代理（来自 \`${short}\`）`
  }
  return `📨 子代理 → 主代理（助手）· 汇报/回话（来自 \`${short}\`）`
}

/** session-xxxx / 裸 id 统一成短 id。 */
function shortSessionId(id) {
  return String(id ?? '').replace(/^session-/, '').slice(0, 8) || '未知'
}
```

  const known = sender ? roster?.get(sender) : undefined
  const who = sender
    ? (known ? `${isSubagentSession(known) ? '子代理' : '主代理'} \`${shortSessionId(sender)}\`` : `代理会话 \`${shortSessionId(sender)}\``)
    : '未知代理会话'
  const self = header?.id ?? ''
  const selfWho = isSubagentSession(header) ? '本子代理' : '主代理（助手）'
  ... 
}
```

`📨 代理间消息（不是人发的）：来自 <who>` where `<who>` = `子代理 <short>` / `主代理 <short>` / `代理会话 <short>`.

And for the "who is this session" part, the file header already states 会话类型. So:

```js
function agentRelayLabel(message, header, roster) {
  const sender = message?.source?.senderSessionId
  const senderHeader = sender ? roster?.get(sender) : undefined
  const senderName = senderHeader
    ? `${isSubagentSession(senderHeader) ? '子代理' : '主代理（助手）'} \`${shortSessionId(sender)}\``
    : sender ? `代理会话 \`${shortSessionId(sender)}\`` : '未知来源'
  const senderTitle = senderHeader ? titleOf(senderHeader) : ''   // roster 里存了标题吗？
  return `📨 代理间消息（不是人发的）：${senderName}${senderTitle ? `「${senderTitle}」` : ''} → 本会话`
}
```

OK, final:
```js
function agentRelayLabel(message, header, roster) {
  const sender = message?.source?.senderSessionId
  const known = sender ? roster?.get(sender) : undefined
  const who = known
    ? (isSubagentSession(known) ? `子代理 \`${shortSessionId(sender)}\`` : `主代理（助手）\`${shortSessionId(sender)}\``)
    : (sender ? `代理会话 \`${shortSessionId(sender)}\`` : '未知代理会话')
  return `📨 代理间消息（不是人发的）：${who} → 本会话`
}
```

That's precise and always correct. 

### stripRelayPrefix
The relay body starts with `"Agent <id> sent a message: "`. Let me strip it:
```js
/** 去掉代理中继消息自带的「Agent xxx sent a message: 」前缀。 */
function stripRelayPrefix(text) {
  return text.replace(/^Agent\s+\S+\s+sent a message:\s*/i, '').trim()
}
```
Hmm, blockText joins the two text parts with '\n\n', so the text becomes `"Agent xxx sent a message: \n\n<body>"`. The regex with `\s*` handles the newlines. Good.

```js
/** 非人类消息（代理间往来）正文太长时折叠；人类消息从不折叠。 */
function renderAgentish(text, origin) {
  if (origin.role === 'human' || text.length <= AGENT_BODY_INLINE_CHARS) return text
  return [`<details><summary>展开消息正文（${text.length} 字）</summary>`, '', fence(text), '', '</details>'].join('\n')
}
```
with `const AGENT_BODY_INLINE_CHARS = 2000`.

Hmm, using `fence` inside details — a code fence keeps long text from being mangled. But for a subagent delegation prompt, a code fence is fine.

/** 「对话正文」：人类消息 + 助手回复正文 + 助手与子代理之间的往来消息，每条都有明确署名。 */
function renderConversation(header, events, meta, roster) {
  const sub = isSubagentSession(header)
  const out = []
  out.push(`# 对话正文：${meta.title || '(无标题)'}`)
  out.push('')
  out.push(`- 会话 ID：\`${header.id}\``)
  out.push(`- 创建时间：${localTime(header.createdAt)}`)
  out.push(`- 会话类型：${sub ? `**子代理会话**（主代理委派，父会话 \`${header.parentSession ?? '未知'}\`）` : '**主对话**（你与助手的对话）'}`)
  out.push(`- 归档时间：${localTime(Date.now())}`)
  out.push(`- 轮次：${meta.turns}`)
  out.push('')
  out.push('**署名约定**')
  out.push('')
  out.push('| 署名 | 含义 |')
  out.push('| --- | --- |')
  out.push('| 👤 用户（本人） | 人在 GUI 里亲手发的消息 |')
  out.push('| 🤖 助手 | 助手（我）的回复正文 |')
  out.push('| 🧑💻 助手 → 子代理 | 助手派给子代理的任务 / 追加消息（**不是人发的**） |')
  out.push('| 📨 代理间消息 | 代理之间中转的消息，如子代理汇报（**不是人发的**） |')
  out.push('')
  out.push('> 工具调用、思考过程、系统注入上下文见同名的「会话全文」。')
  out.push('> 由 `code/tools/dsh-archive.mjs` 自动生成，请勿手工编辑。')
  out.push('')
  ...
}
```

Now the body loop:

```js
  let turn = 0
  let pending = []
  const flush = () => { ... same ... }

  for (const event of events) {
    const data = event.data ?? {}
    if (event.type === 'turn/start') { ... same ... }
    if (event.type === 'user/message') {
      const message = data.message ?? data
      const origin = messageOrigin(message, header, roster)
      if (origin.role === 'system') continue
      const raw = blockText(message.content)
      const text = origin.role === 'agent' ? stripRelayPrefix(raw) : raw.trim()
      if (!text.trim()) continue
      flush()
      out.push(`### ${origin.label}`)
      out.push('')
      out.push(renderAgentish(text.trim(), origin))
      out.push('')
      continue
    }
    if (event.type === 'assistant/message') {
      const message = data.message ?? {}
      for (const part of message.content ?? []) {
        if (part.type === 'text' && part.text?.trim()) {
          pending.push(part.text.trim())
          continue
        }
        if (part.type === 'tool-call' && DELEGATION_TOOLS.has(part.name)) {
          flush()
          out.push(...renderDelegation(part))
          continue
        }
      }
      continue
    }
  }
  flush()
  return out.join('\n')
}
```

`renderDelegation(part)`:
```js
/** 助手发给子代理的委派/追加消息：在对话正文里也留下带署名的痕迹。 */
function renderDelegation(part) {
  const args = parseToolArgs(part.arguments)
  const body = typeof args.prompt === 'string' ? args.prompt : (typeof args.message === 'string' ? args.message : '')
  const target = typeof args.description === 'string' && args.description.trim()
    ? args.description.trim()
    : (typeof args.agent_id === 'string' ? `会话 \`${shortSessionId(args.agent_id)}\`` : '')
  const title = DELEGATION_TOOLS.get(part.name)
  const out = []
  out.push(`### 🧑💻 助手 → 子代理 · ${title}`)
  out.push('')
  out.push(`- 工具：\`${part.name}\``)
  if (target) out.push(`- 对象：${target}`)
  if (!body.trim()) out.push('- 正文：无')
  out.push('')
  if (body.trim()) {
    out.push(`<details><summary>展开发给子代理的消息正文（${body.length} 字，**不是人发的**）</summary>`)
    out.push('')
    out.push(fence(truncate(body.trim(), MAX_DELEGATION_CHARS, 'delegation truncated')))
    out.push('')
    out.push('</details>')
    out.push('')
  }
  return out
}
```
with `MAX_DELEGATION_CHARS = 12000`.

`parseToolArgs`:
```js
/** 工具参数可能是 JSON 字符串或对象，统一成对象。 */
function parseToolArgs(raw) {
  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object') return raw
  if (typeof raw !== 'string') return {}
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
    return parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' ? parsed : {}
  } catch {
    return {}
  }
}
```

DELEGATION_TOOLS:
```js
/** 助手用来跟子代理说话的工具：这些调用的正文要在「对话正文」里署名出现。 */

  ['subagent_fork', '委派任务（fork 子代理）'],
  ['send_message', '追加消息'],
  ['interrupt_agent', '打断子代理'],
])
```
